# FINDINGS

## Corrections to my round-3 report

**1. Finding A: the defect is real, but its impact is a missing audit/outcome record and a Replay mismatch, not a lost trade.**
- Both Codex and I said a candle closed by the startup REST import becomes "ineligible for close dispatch". That is true, but under the intended design it was never dispatchable anyway.
  - A candle that closes while the trader is down is historical-only. The correct recovery path also never dispatches it: it records `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE` (`LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:189`).
- What is actually wrong with the import path:
  - It closes the existing open row but leaves its provenance as `RUNTIME` with a NULL token (`CandleRepository.java:173-188`). A historical row is stored looking like a live one.
  - It writes no audit row and no terminal outcome, which breaks AGENTS.md's "one durable terminal outcome" rule.
  - Replay selects closed candles by time, so it will include this row even though production never analysed it.
- How often it happens is an inference: a 1m candle will be affected on most deploys, because the drain wait can be up to 120 s (`Deploy-Trader.ps1:112`). No measured downtime exists.

**2. My suggested fix for A was incomplete.**
- I suggested routing rows left open at shutdown through the update-only recovery path. That cannot work as things stand:
  - Recovery only looks at open rows (`closed=0`, `LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:131`).
  - It starts after a 15 s delay (`application.yml:45`).
  - The startup import (`@Order(10)` runner, `MarketDataBootstrapService.java:27,96`) has already closed those rows by then.
- So switching reconciliation on would change nothing for A. Any fix also needs the import to leave non-`BACKFILL` open rows alone.

**3. The comment contradiction is wider than one line.** Besides `CandleRepository.java:205`, two more comments contradict the code:
- `MarketDataBootstrapService.java:23-24` says only missing rows are inserted.
- `CandleRepository.java:127-130` says only recovery avoids stealing a live close.

## Challenges to Codex

- **Codex objection 11 (claude-docs says direct ingestion is disabled in LIVE and OBSERVE) is wrong.** `claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:47-50` says "disabled in LIVE". The real errors are elsewhere:
  - It groups OBSERVE with LIVE under "`SharedAnalysisDispatcher` drives analysis".
  - It says "The two paths must never run together", but OBSERVE runs both.
- **Codex finding 7 (OBSERVE startup trading) is confirmed, with a limit.**
  - The bootstrap analysis and paper-trading step is skipped only in OFF (`MarketDataBootstrapService.java:107`).
  - The shared processing path checks freshness on every attempt regardless of where the signal came from (`SignalProcessingCoordinator.java:97-99`).
  - Freshness only requires the newest closed candle within its grace period (`SignalProcessingFreshness.java:31-38`).
  - Strategy and risk rules still run afterwards, so a trade is possible, not certain.
- **Codex findings 5, 6, 9 and 10 are re-confirmed.** Codex's "not every restart" qualifier is correct.

## New findings this round

- **V103 changes the collector's schema, not just checks that it exists.**
  - It confirms the history database is `crypto_ai`, then adds indexes to `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event` (`V103...java:39-58`).
  - This is a startup migration rather than runtime access, so it isn't strictly the runtime rule AGENTS.md states. It still couples the trader to the collector's schema more tightly than "requires the table".
- **B105 may be silently ignored.**
  - The trader-only baseline depends on Flyway honouring `B`-prefixed migrations. The project uses stock `spring-boot-starter-flyway` and `flyway-mysql` with no edition setting (`pom.xml:57-66`).
  - I believe baseline migrations may be limited to paid Flyway editions. If the edition in use doesn't support them, B105 is skipped without error, V103 runs, and a fresh install fails. This is unverified.
- **No test catches the duplicate `local-candle:` key.**
  - The key appears twice (`application.yml:40` and `:447`).
  - The only test that touches the file reads it as raw text (`DeploymentSafetyContractTest.java:14`). Nothing starts Spring with it (no `@SpringBootTest`).
  - I could not check Boot 4.1.0's YAML loader: the local sources jar is compressed and can't be searched with these tools.
- **The bundled defaults auto-approve a LIVE cutover.**
  - `activation-approved` and `automatic-first-cutover-enabled` both default to true (`application.yml:422-424`).
  - Only the deploy script forces `activation-approved=false` (`Deploy-Trader.ps1:127`). A manual or IDE start with `SHARED_MARKET_MODE=LIVE` would inherit approval.
- **There is no runtime evidence that this code has ever run.** `logs/` contains no FIX-153 markers (`BACKFILL_IMPORT_RETURNED`, `DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED`) and no duplicate-key error. Every behaviour claim here comes from source.

## Unchanged (re-verified)

| Mode | Kline WebSocket | REST import / bootstrap | Candle reads and writes | Collector |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **OFF** (default; forced by deploy) | Trader | Trader | Local `crypto_ai.candle` | Not used |
| **LIVE** | Refused (`BinanceKlineService.java:56`) | Refused (`MarketDataService.java:32`) | Reads from collector `crypto_ai_v2`; local writes refused | Supplies candles |
| **OBSERVE** | Trader | Trader | Local | Connection pool and consumer both active |

- A close token is assigned only when a row goes from open to closed (`CandleRepository.java:201`).
- Only the receipt that owns that token publishes close analysis (`BinanceKlineService.java:110-111`). Phase order is input → protection → observer → close dispatch.
- The detector only takes closed rows with a token that are not `BACKFILL`, and only looks back 180–600 s (`LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:47-50`).

# OBJECTIONS (unresolved, material)
1. **The startup import and the manual/coin-activation import close open `RUNTIME` rows** with no audit row, no outcome and no provenance change. Switching reconciliation on does not fix this.
2. **OBSERVE in the main trader** runs direct ingestion, startup paper trading and collector consumption together, without any of the OFF-only safeguards. Nothing in the trader refuses OBSERVE.
3. **Reconciliation** is disabled, has no terminal age-out, and applies `LIMIT 50` before its due/evidence filtering.
4. **The detector keeps its retry state only in memory** with a short lookback, so after a longer outage some candles get no outcome.
5. **No shared Binance REST rate-limit or ban handling** across bootstrap, reconciliation and clock calls.
6. **Replay parity is not shown:** Replay selects by time, not by provenance or outcome.
7. **Fresh trader-only install is unproven.** V103 needs and alters `crypto_ai_v2`, and B105 may not be honoured by this Flyway edition.
8. **Duplicate `local-candle:` key** with no test that loads the configuration. Possible startup failure.
9. **Documentation drift:**
   - AGENTS.md calls the design "pending", but it is the code default.
   - claude-docs misdescribes OBSERVE and omits that backfill closes open rows.
   - Three source comments contradict the SQL they describe.

YOUR PRIOR FINDINGS:
## FINDINGS

1. **Implemented default is trader-owned local ingestion, not the pending-only direction.** Checked-in `shared-market.mode` defaults to `OFF`; in OFF the trader starts Binance WebSocket ingestion, performs REST bootstrap/import, and persists to its local `crypto_ai.candle`.

2. **LIVE retains collector ownership.** In LIVE, trader WebSocket and REST ingestion throw/return early, candle reads route through the collector read pool targeting `crypto_ai_v2`, and local writes are rejected.

3. **OBSERVE is an architectural contradiction.** It is not collector-only:
   - Direct trader WebSocket and REST ingestion remain enabled because their guard is only `LIVE`.
   - It also creates a read-only collector pool and the shared consumer polls collector tables.
   - Bootstrap may generate `STARTUP` signals and call paper-trading processing in OBSERVE.
   - The direct-price pending resolver is OFF-only, so direct 1m OBSERVE events can remain `PENDING`, causing Replay to reject parity claims.

   The other report’s statement that OBSERVE “trades exactly as OFF” is too strong: code establishes that it can enter the analysis/paper-trading path, but a completed trade still depends on downstream strategy/risk/execution gates not traced here.

4. **Runtime close handling has the intended basic shape.** The runtime upsert assigns a close token only for an open→closed transition. The successful token-owning receipt publishes after input → protection → observer ordering, and async dispatcher work occurs after commit.

5. **Ordinary REST import violates the pending recovery/backfill contract.** Its `BACKFILL` duplicate-key clause can finalize an existing `closed=0` candle. It preserves provenance and adds no token, no analysis outcome, and no dispatch. Therefore a candle left open during shutdown can be closed by bootstrap before WebSocket startup and become ineligible for both normal token dispatch and reconciliation (which selects only `closed=0` rows).

6. **The implemented reconciliation slice is safer than ordinary REST import, but disabled and incomplete.** It is OFF-only and disabled by default. Its recovery SQL is update-only for existing open rows; gap repair is insert-if-absent; both produce `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE` outcomes when successful. However:
   - candidate selection applies `LIMIT 50` before due/loss-evidence filtering;
   - only active-window candidates are selected, so a transient `PENDING` candidate that ages out is no longer selected and has no terminal age-out path;
   - no shared REST rate-limit/ban policy was found.

7. **Replay is not proven to reproduce the local live-admission boundary.** Replay loads any `closed=1` candles by time/identity, without filtering by runtime provenance, close token, reconciliation outcome, or historical-only status. It does correctly reject unresolved `PENDING` price evidence, but that is narrower than candle-admission parity.

8. **Fresh trader-only installation remains unresolved.** The applied Java migration `V103` explicitly fails unless `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event` exists, contradicting a fully collector-independent fresh bootstrap.

9. **Documentation conflicts with code and with itself.**
   - `AGENTS.md` calls local-candle runtime a pending design, while code defaults to OFF and both architecture documents say FIX-153 is implemented in source but unapproved.
   - `claude-docs` says direct ingestion is disabled in both LIVE and OBSERVE; code disables it only in LIVE.
   - `codex-docs` accurately describes ordinary REST finalization, but its adjacent “pending reconciliation” narrative is stale relative to the implemented, disabled reconciliation slice.
   - `CandleRepository` claims REST backfill “inserts missing identities only,” contradicted by its duplicate-key update SQL.

## Trade investigations
When asked to investigate a trade:

- Confirm the application, environment, symbol, and trade ID.
- Use configured database and SSH connections without displaying secrets.
- Use read-only, bounded queries and targeted log searches.
- Read the actual schema before choosing tables or columns.
- Reconstruct the timeline: candle, signal, decision, entry, protection,
  and exit. Display timestamps in KSA (UTC+03:00), interpreting stored
  timestamps as UTC.
- Trace the relevant code and configuration for each decision.
- Check data freshness, entry quality, strategy, risk/reward, fees,
  stop-loss, and exit reason where applicable.
- Assess the entry using only information available at that moment.
  Use subsequent price action separately to evaluate the outcome.
- Distinguish a valid losing trade from a logic defect, stale data,
  execution delay, configuration issue, or missing evidence.
- Check whether the behavior can be reproduced in Replay.
- Support findings with record IDs, log timestamps, and code references.
- State uncertainties; do not invent a cause.
- Do not change code, configuration, services, or production data during
  an investigation unless the user requests a fix.

Report:
1. What happened.
2. Why the system entered and exited.
3. Whether the behavior followed the intended rules.
4. Confirmed defects and missing evidence.
5. Recommended correction and regression checks, if needed.

## Current architecture direction (pending implementation)

The current priority is to remove the trader's runtime dependency on the
collector. This is a design decision, not an implemented/deployed change.

- Target runtime: trader-owned Binance kline ingestion and local
  `crypto_ai.candle` writes with `shared-market.mode=OFF`.
- The collector remains independent in `crypto_ai_v2`; the trader must not
  connect to, read from, or write collector stream/cursor tables at runtime.
- Do not run direct ingestion and the shared-market executors together.
- Applied shared-market tables and Flyway history are historical evidence; do
  not drop, reset, or edit them merely to enable the new path.
- `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` still requires
  `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event` for a fresh Flyway install. Treat
  trader-only bootstrap as a release blocker until a forward, tested installer
  policy exists. Never edit an applied Flyway migration.

## Local-candle implementation safety

When implementing or reviewing the local-candle design:

- Keep `shared-market.fix142.enabled=false`,
  `shared-market.fix142.acknowledgment-enabled=false`, and scheduled analysis
  disabled for the OFF-mode cutover unless a reviewed change explicitly says
  otherwise.
- Preserve the input -> protection -> observer -> close-dispatch transaction
  order. Candle close analysis must not run on the Binance ingestion thread.
- A closed candle needs durable/recoverable analysis admission; an in-memory
  event/queue alone loses work across a crash. Recovery must distinguish fresh
  execution-capable work from historical-only analysis.
- Candle updates must be monotonic: a late open update cannot reopen or
  overwrite a closed candle. Publish close work only for an atomic
  open-to-closed transition.
- A WebSocket reconnect can occur at a candle-close boundary and Binance may
  not replay that final closed-kline event. Candle recovery is automatic: use
  an exact authoritative Binance REST candle only after exchange-time
  finality and the measured live-close grace. It may either finalise a matching
  existing `closed=0` row or insert a wholly absent identity inside the active
  data-quality window. A recovered open row preserves provenance; an inserted
  gap row uses `BACKFILL` provenance. Neither path may alter a completed row,
  create a close-transition token, dispatch analysis, create a live signal,
  or execute a trade. The recovery must have durable retry/audit evidence,
  bounded rate-limit handling, health visibility, and no manual repair step.
- Automation direction: no pending candle or analysis outcome may require
  manual approval, manual database repair, or a persisted trading fence.
  Reconciliation must retry and resolve automatically, or age out to a durable
  explicit outcome with a visible reason. This does not permit unverified data
  to masquerade as valid or force an entry: a genuine strategy/risk veto or
  unavailable authoritative data remains non-executable evidence, not a
  silently stuck pending signal.
- Gap backfill must be a pure insert-if-absent operation. It must never use a
  duplicate-key update that could finalise or alter a live row which appeared
  while the authoritative REST request was in progress. Recovery candidates
  are bounded by the active quality window and terminal audit outcomes are not
  retried. Each candle/work path must write one durable terminal analysis
  outcome; transient failures remain retryable and write no terminal outcome.
- Any future universal trading-mutation barrier is an in-memory drain
  participant only. Outside an active drain it must not block, delay, queue,
  or persistently fence fresh trading, signals, or protection. It may reject
  new mutations only while draining and must then wait for admitted mutations
  to finish before shutdown.
- Historical-only recovery must be structurally unable to reach execution,
  wallet, position, order, or executable-work services. Quarantined work with
  possible financial effects may be concluded automatically only from proven
  durable effect evidence (`EFFECT_COMMITTED` or `EFFECT_ABSENT`); unknown
  atomicity remains a release blocker. Binance REST rate-limit/ban handling
  must be shared by reconciliation, clock sync, bootstrap, and other REST
  callers.
- Enforce freshness from persisted candle close time before analysis and again
  before execution. Historical-only signals must be explicitly marked and
  excluded from live-latency monitoring.
- Price protection must receive the Binance observation timestamp and validate
  age/order inside the protection transaction. Paper-trading effects and their
  accepted price-event state must commit atomically; any future live-exchange
  effect needs a separate idempotent design.
- Do not add manual approval, persisted review fences, or database locks that
  block new analysis, signals, entries, or protection unless the user changes
  that direction. In-process coordination and normal database constraints are
  distinct from persisted trading fences.
- OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must not query or write retired shared-market
  tables. Existing `REVIEW_REQUIRED` history must not block OFF-mode work.
- Keep production and Replay evidence aligned. Replay must fail a parity claim
  for unresolved direct price-event evidence, while deliberate exclusions stay
  excluded.

## Delivery discipline

- Treat the local-candle architecture as not deployment-ready until its
  migration/bootstrap policy, concurrency, crash/restart, stale-input,
  Replay-parity, and Java 21/MySQL validation gates pass.
- Do not deploy or change production configuration/data unless the user
  explicitly requests it.
- Protection coverage for open positions during application replacement remains
  an unresolved release gate. Do not represent the local-candle design as
  deployment-ready until it is implemented or conclusively disproven.

## FIX-154: pending higher-timeframe BUY validation

FIX-154 is a separate, not-yet-implemented design. Do not merge it into the
local-candle change or describe it as deployed.

Purpose: a historical bearish 1h signal may block or delay a current 1m BUY
only when its own evidence is fresh, data-quality-valid, and sufficiently
strong. A historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` must remain investigatory
evidence, not a higher-timeframe validity gate; the current 1m signal's own
BTC protection remains unchanged.

- Score usable bearish 1h evidence into `NOT_TRUSTED`, `SOFT_CONFLICT`, or
  `HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE` using closed-candle local indicators.
- A hard veto requires strong 1h evidence plus fresh, quality-valid bearish
  5m confirmation. Stale, invalid, or unknown historical quality can never
  hard-veto.
- Persist `trade_signal.data_quality_valid` from
  `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new signal. Historical NULL
  means unknown/invalid; never backfill it to true.
- A confirmed 1h recovery may release only a soft conflict and only with fresh,
  quality-valid, non-bearish 5m evidence.
- Preserve the legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` behavior: the new data-quality gate
  applies to the new hard-veto/recovery paths, not that legacy fallback.
- Add immutable BUY-evaluation audit evidence; maintain Replay parity and
  prevent future-candle or wall-clock look-ahead.

63:     }
64:     public boolean isIdle(){return !running.get();}
65:     public Map<String,Object> health(){
66:         Map<String,Object> result=new LinkedHashMap<>();result.put("enabled",enabled);result.put("inFlight",running.get());
67:         try {
68:             result.put("pendingCandidates",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='PENDING'",Long.class));
69:             result.put("recoveredCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='RECOVERED'",Long.class));
70:             result.put("gapBackfilledCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='GAP_BACKFILLED'",Long.class));
71:             result.put("expiredCount",jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit WHERE status='EXPIRED'",Long.class));
72:             Instant local=Instant.now();Instant reference=exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);
73:             List<Map<String,Object>> activeGaps=new ArrayList<>();
74:             for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols())for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()){
75:                 Instant lower=reference.minusSeconds(Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*intervalSeconds(interval));
76:                 Timestamp first=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT MIN(open_time) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=?",Timestamp.class,symbol,interval);
77:                 if(first==null)continue;
78:                 Long count=jdbc.queryForObject("""
79:                     SELECT COUNT(*) FROM local_reconciliation_audit
80:                     WHERE subject_type='CANDLE_DATA' AND symbol=? AND interval_code=?
81:                       AND status='EXPIRED' AND failure_reason='NOT_ON_EXCHANGE'
82:                       AND candle_open_time>=? AND candle_open_time>?
83:                     """,Long.class,symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lower),first);
84:                 if(count!=null&&count>0)activeGaps.add(Map.of("symbol",symbol,"interval",interval,"count",count));
85:             }
86:             result.put("exchangeGapBlockedByLane",activeGaps);
87:             result.put("exchangeGapBlockedCount",activeGaps.stream().mapToLong(row->((Number)row.get("count")).longValue()).sum());
88:         }catch(RuntimeException unavailable){result.put("status","NOT_MIGRATED_OR_UNAVAILABLE");}
89:         return result;
90:     }
91: 
92:     private void request(String reason){
93:         if(!enabled || !"OFF".equals(mode) || !running.compareAndSet(false,true))return;
94:         try { executor.execute(()-> { try { runPass(reason); } finally { running.set(false); } }); }
95:         catch(RuntimeException rejected){running.set(false);log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_REJECTED] reason={}",reason,rejected);}
96:     }
97: 
98:     private void runPass(String reason){
99:         if(drain!=null&&!drain.beginReconciliation())return;
100:         try {
101:             // The server-time read happens before every REST kline request and is
102:             // the sole exchange-time reference for this pass.
103:             Instant exchangeNow=binance.getServerTime();
104:             Instant databaseNow=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)",Timestamp.class).toInstant();
105:             int[] remaining={Math.max(0,requestBudget)};
106:             for(Candidate candidate:openCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
107:                 if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
108:                 if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
109:                 recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,false);
110:             }
111:             if(remaining[0]>0) for(Candidate candidate:gapCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
112:                 if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
113:                 if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
114:                 recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,true);
115:             }
116:             log.debug("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS] reason={}, exchangeNow={}, budget={}",reason,exchangeNow,requestBudget-remaining[0]);
117:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
118:             log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS_FAILED] reason={}; no candle was treated as recovered",reason,failure);
119:         } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeReconciliation();}
120:     }
121: 
122:     private List<Candidate> openCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
123:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
124:         for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
125:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
126:             Instant lower=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window);
127:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
128:             Instant latestOpen=eligible.minusSeconds(seconds);
129:             result.addAll(jdbc.query("""
130:                 SELECT symbol,interval_code,open_time FROM candle
131:                 WHERE closed=0 AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<=?
132:                   AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
133:                 ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 50
134:                 """,(rs,n)->{
135:                     Instant open=rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant();
136:                     return new Candidate(rs.getString(1),rs.getString(2),open,expectedClose(open,rs.getString(2)));
137:                 },interval,Timestamp.from(lower),Timestamp.from(latestOpen)));
138:         }
139:         return result.stream().filter(c->audit.due(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,databaseNow))
140:             .filter(this::positiveLossEvidence).toList();
141:     }
142: 
143:     /** Read-only discovery of absent expected identities in the active quality window. */
144:     private List<Candidate> gapCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
145:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
146:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
147:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
148:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
149:             Instant first=Instant.ofEpochSecond((exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window).getEpochSecond()/seconds)*seconds);
150:             Set<Instant> present=new HashSet<>(jdbc.query("""
151:                 SELECT open_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<?
152:                 """,(rs,n)->rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(first),Timestamp.from(eligible)));
153:             for(Instant open=first;open.plusSeconds(seconds).compareTo(eligible)<=0;open=open.plusSeconds(seconds)) {
154:                 if(!present.contains(open) && audit.due(symbol,interval,open,databaseNow))
155:                     result.add(new Candidate(symbol,interval,open,open.plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1)));
156:             }
157:         }
158:         return result;
159:     }
160: 
161:     private void recover(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow,boolean gap) {
162:         var prior=audit.findCandle(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
163:         int attempts=prior.map(s->s.attempts()+1).orElse(1);
164:         try {
165:             List<BinanceKline> rows=binance.getKlines(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,candidate.closeTime);
166:             List<BinanceKline> exact=rows.stream().filter(k->k.openTime().equals(candidate.openTime)&&k.closeTime().equals(candidate.closeTime)).toList();
167:             if(rows.isEmpty() && gap) {
168:                 List<BinanceKline> first=binance.getKlinesAtOrAfter(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
169:                 if(!first.isEmpty() && first.getFirst().openTime().isAfter(candidate.openTime)) {
170:                     terminalUnavailable(candidate,exchangeNow,"NOT_ON_EXCHANGE");return;
171:                 }
172:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return;
173:             }
174:             if(rows.isEmpty()) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return; }
175:             if(candidate.closeTime.compareTo(exchangeNow)>=0) {
176:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_NOT_FINAL",exchangeNow);return;
177:             }
178:             if(exact.size()!=1) {
179:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_MISMATCH",exchangeNow);return;
180:             }
181:             BinanceKline k=exact.getFirst();
182:             tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
183:                 boolean changed=gap ? candles.insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
184:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
185:                     : candles.finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
186:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume());
187:                 String result=changed ? (gap?"GAP_BACKFILLED":"RECOVERED") : (gap?"ALREADY_PRESENT":"ALREADY_CLOSED");
188:                 audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,result,null,exchangeNow,k.openTime(),k.closeTime());
189:                 if(changed) outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE",result,null,gap?"AUTHORITATIVE_GAP_BACKFILL":"AUTHORITATIVE_RECOVERY");
190:             });
191:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_FAILURE",exchangeNow); }
192:     }
193: 
194:     private void pending(Candidate c,int attempts,Instant databaseNow,String reason,Instant exchangeNow){
195:         long delay=Math.min(300,attempts==1?5:attempts==2?15:60L*(1L<<Math.min(2,attempts-3)));
196:         audit.pending(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,attempts,databaseNow.plusSeconds(delay),reason,exchangeNow);
197:     }
198:     private boolean positiveLossEvidence(Candidate candidate){
199:         // This relies on the current FIX-131 invariant: one primary WebSocket
200:         // session invokes processKline synchronously and in Binance receive
201:         // order. If streams are sharded or processing becomes asynchronous,
202:         // this successor proof must be redesigned and re-tested.
203:         Instant successor=candidate.openTime.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(candidate.interval));
204:         Integer successorPresent=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",Integer.class,
205:             candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,Timestamp.from(successor));
206:         return successorPresent!=null&&successorPresent>0 || (lastReconnectAdmission!=null && !lastReconnectAdmission.isBefore(candidate.closeTime));
207:     }
208:     private void terminalUnavailable(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,String reason){
209:         tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
210:             audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"EXPIRED",reason,exchangeNow,null,null);
211:             outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",reason,null,"AUTHORITATIVE_RECONCILIATION");
212:         });
213:     }
214:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported interval: "+interval);};}
215:     private static Instant expectedClose(Instant open,String interval){return open.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)).minusMillis(1);}
216:     private record Candidate(String symbol,String interval,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime) {}
217: }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java
1: package com.crypto.shared;
2: 
3: import com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariConfig;
4: import com.zaxxer.hikari.HikariDataSource;
5: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
6: import org.springframework.core.env.Environment;
7: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
9: 
10: /** FIX-132: separate SELECT-only pool; never register a second DataSource bean
11:  * that would accidentally replace Boot's local Flyway/JPA datasource. */
12: @Component
13: public class SharedMarketSource {
14:     private final boolean enabled;
15:     private final String mode;
16:     private final JdbcTemplate local;
17:     private final JdbcTemplate reader;
18:     private final HikariDataSource pool;
19:     public SharedMarketSource(JdbcTemplate local, Environment env) {
20:         mode = env.getProperty("shared-market.mode", "OFF").trim();
21:         if (!java.util.Set.of("OFF","OBSERVE","LIVE").contains(mode)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-132 invalid shared-market.mode");
22:         this.local=local; enabled="LIVE".equals(mode);
23:         if ("OFF".equals(mode)) { reader = local; pool = null; return; }
24:         HikariConfig c = new HikariConfig();
25:         c.setJdbcUrl(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.jdbc-url"));
26:         c.setUsername(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.username"));
27:         c.setPassword(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.password"));
28:         c.setMaximumPoolSize(3); c.setMinimumIdle(0); c.setReadOnly(true);
29:         c.setConnectionTimeout(5000); c.setValidationTimeout(2000); c.setConnectionInitSql("SET time_zone = '+00:00'");
30:         c.setPoolName("fix132-source-read");
31:         // Finite driver waits as well as pool/query waits; no single overall deadline is claimed.
32:         c.addDataSourceProperty("connectTimeout", "2000");
33:         c.addDataSourceProperty("socketTimeout", "5000");
34:         c.addDataSourceProperty("connectionTimeZone","UTC");
35:         c.addDataSourceProperty("forceConnectionTimeZoneToSession","true");
36:         pool = new HikariDataSource(c); reader = new JdbcTemplate(env.getProperty("diagnostics.jdbc.enabled",Boolean.class,true) ? new com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource(pool,"collector-read",env.getProperty("diagnostics.jdbc.slow-ms",Long.class,250L)) : pool); reader.setQueryTimeout(5);
37:         try {
38:             // FIX-149: validate the explicitly configured collector schema, distinct from the trader database.
39:             String expectedSchema = env.getProperty("shared-market.source-schema", "crypto_ai_v2");
40:             if(!expectedSchema.equals(reader.queryForObject("SELECT DATABASE()",String.class)))
41:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 shared connection must target configured source schema " + expectedSchema);
42:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pool.close();throw failure; }
43:     }
44:     public boolean enabled() { return enabled; }
45:     public JdbcTemplate reader() { return enabled ? reader : local; }
46:     public JdbcTemplate feedReader() { return reader; }
47:     /** Instantaneous pool pressure, not a per-query connection-wait measurement. */
48:     public java.util.Map<String,Integer> poolPressure() {
49:         if(pool==null || pool.getHikariPoolMXBean()==null)return java.util.Map.of();
50:         var bean=pool.getHikariPoolMXBean();
51:         return java.util.Map.of("active",bean.getActiveConnections(),"idle",bean.getIdleConnections(),"waiting",bean.getThreadsAwaitingConnection());
52:     }
53:     public String mode() { return mode; }
54:     public void requireLocalWriter() {
55:         if (enabled) throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132: collector owns candle writes; Trader import is disabled");
56:     }
57:     @PreDestroy public void close() { if (pool != null) pool.close(); }
58: }

20:  * Each phase fences on the local symbol row through COMMIT, then releases all

22:  * Durable rows are the backlog; executors never hold an unbounded event queue. */

121:     @org.springframework.context.event.EventListener(org.springframework.boot.context.event.ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
122:     public void start() {
123:         if(fix142Enabled && !source.enabled())throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-142 live scheduling requires LIVE mode");
124:         if(admissionCeiling<2 || freshReserve<1 || freshReserve>=admissionCeiling)
125:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-142 invalid admission ceiling/reserve");
126:         protocol.ceiling=admissionCeiling;protocol.reserve=freshReserve;
127:         analysisDispatch.historicalCapacity(historicalThreads);
128:         analysisDispatch.historicalEnabled(historicalEnabled);
129:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled(ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled);
130:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds(ownerTimeoutReconciliationQuietSeconds);
131:         // FIX-146: do not throw ApplicationReadyEvent for recoverable symbol state.
132:         // Every dispatch surface uses this gate; diagnostics and operator APIs stay up.
133:         initializeTradingReadiness();
134:         analysisDispatch.acknowledgmentEnabled(acknowledgmentEnabled);
135:         analysisDispatch.freshPriority(fix142Enabled);
136:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
137:         if(fix142Enabled) {
138:             freshClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discoverFresh,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
139:             protectionClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchProtectionTicks,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
140:         }

142:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
143:         if(source.enabled())readinessClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::refreshTradingReadiness,10,10,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
144:         // Independent clocks: slow source reads or CallerRuns analysis saturation
145:         // cannot monopolize Spring's default scheduler and stop price dispatch.
146:         discoveryClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discover,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
147:         priceClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchPrices,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
148:         analysisClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchAnalysis,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
149:         if(historicalEnabled)historyClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchHistoricalAnalysis,1000,1000,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
150:         log.info("[FIX-140][HISTORY_CONFIGURATION] enabled={}, savedSignalsAndCandlesRetained=true",historicalEnabled);
151:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::monitor,30,30,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
152:         // FIX-142 review correction: old protection attempts age out of the recent-price
153:         // selector, so fencing/alerting must not depend on another claim selecting them.
154:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::monitorProtectionAttempts,5,5,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
155:         // FIX-141 Phase 2: runs independently of claim()'s REVIEW_BATCH_LIMIT check, so a lane
156:         // that crossed 16 unresolved reviews can still drain over successive runs.
157:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileAnalysisOnlyReviews,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
158:         // FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: owner-timeout reconciliation is diagnostic-only
159:         // (logs candidates, performs no mutation) and off until the team enables it and sets a
160:         // quiet window (see SharedAnalysisDispatcher#ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled/Seconds);
161:         // scheduling it unconditionally here is safe since it no-ops while disabled, and even
162:         // enabled it cannot change analysis_status itself.
163:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews,300,300,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
164:         log.info("[FIX-138][ANALYSIS_CONFIGURATION] liveThreads=8, historyThreads={}, independentClocks=true, livePriority=true, historicalWalletExecution=false",historicalThreads);
165:     }
166:     public void validateCutover() {
167:         log.info("[FIX-132][SOURCE_CONFIGURATION] mode={}, readPool=3, priceWorkers=2, pendingPerSymbol=200, publicationSource=collector",source.mode());
168:         if(!source.enabled())return;
169:         if(!activationApproved)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 LIVE requires explicit measured cutover acceptance (activation-approved)");
170:         var unattributed=jdbc.queryForList(OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS);
171:         if(!unattributed.isEmpty())throw new IllegalStateException("[FIX-132][UNATTRIBUTED_WORK] reconcile before LIVE: "+unattributed);
172:         var invalidOwners=jdbc.queryForList("""
173:             SELECT w.signal_id,w.status,w.source_event_id FROM signal_processing_work w
174:             LEFT JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id
175:             WHERE w.source_event_id IS NOT NULL AND w.status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED')
176:             AND (d.source_event_id IS NULL OR w.symbol<>d.symbol OR w.interval_code<>d.interval_code
177:                  OR w.candle_open_time<>d.candle_open_time OR d.closed=0
178:                  OR d.analysis_status NOT IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED','EXECUTION_RETRY')) ORDER BY w.signal_id LIMIT 25
179:             """);
180:         if(!invalidOwners.isEmpty())throw new IllegalStateException("[FIX-132][PROCESSING_OWNER_INCONSISTENT] reconcile before LIVE: "+invalidOwners);
181:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols())validateSymbolCutover(symbol);
182:     }
183:     // Shared by strict acceptance checks and per-symbol operational readiness.
184:     private Map<String,Object> validateSymbolCutover(String symbol) {
185:             var state=readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
186:             if(state.isEmpty() || !SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(state.getFirst()))
187:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 missing/unapproved/review-required cutover state for "+symbol);
188:             var boundary=state.getFirst();
189:             if(num(boundary,"cutover_sequence")<0 || num(boundary,"discovered_sequence")<num(boundary,"cutover_sequence"))
190:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 invalid sequence boundary for "+symbol);
191:             Integer approval=readinessJdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_approval WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_sequence=? AND cutover_at=? AND approved_by=? AND approved_at=? AND approval_reference=?",Integer.class,
192:                 symbol,boundary.get("approved_sequence"),boundary.get("approved_cutover_at"),boundary.get("approved_by"),boundary.get("approved_at"),boundary.get("approval_reference"));
193:             if(approval==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 missing approval audit for "+symbol);
194:             Long latest=source.feedReader().queryForObject("SELECT last_sequence FROM market_data_stream_cursor WHERE symbol=?",Long.class,symbol);
195:             if(latest==null || latest<num(state.getFirst(),"discovered_sequence"))throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 source sequence regressed for "+symbol);
196:             Set<String> required=new HashSet<>(intervals.getIntervals());required.add("1m");
197:             for(String interval:required) {
198:                 Long count=source.reader().queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT id FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 LIMIT 300) c",Long.class,symbol,interval);
199:                 if(count==null || count<300)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 incomplete source history: "+symbol+" "+interval);
200:             }
201:             return boundary;
202:     }
203: 
204:     private boolean symbolAllowed(String symbol) {
205:         return !source.enabled() || (readinessSymbols.contains(symbol) || !tradingReadiness.managed())
206:             && tradingReadiness.allowed(symbol);
207:     }
208:     public Map<String,Object> tradingReadiness() {
209:         var snapshot=new LinkedHashMap<String,Object>(tradingReadiness.snapshot());
210:         snapshot.put("configuredSymbols",readinessSymbols);
211:         snapshot.put("automaticFirstCutoverEnabled",automaticFirstCutoverEnabled);
212:         snapshot.put("waitingCutoverDoesNotAutoApprove",!automaticFirstCutoverEnabled);
213:         return snapshot;
214:     }
215:     void initializeTradingReadiness() {
216:         if(!source.enabled())return;
217:         tradingReadiness.begin();
218:         refreshTradingReadiness();
219:         log.warn("[FIX-146][STARTUP_READINESS] {}; process stays available, validated first cutovers may activate automatically, uncertain symbols receive no new shared attempts",tradingReadiness.snapshot());
220:     }
221:     private static final String SYMBOL_LEGACY_BLOCKERS=OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS
222:         .replace("WHERE w.source_event_id IS NULL","WHERE w.symbol=? AND w.source_event_id IS NULL");
223:     private static final String UNSCOPED_BLOCKERS=OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS
224:         .replace("WHERE w.source_event_id IS NULL","WHERE (w.symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(w.symbol)='') AND w.source_event_id IS NULL");
225:     // Dedicated clock and round-robin budget: one slow probe cannot monopolize trading
226:     // clocks or permanently strand later symbols. JDBC timeouts are not total deadlines.
227:     synchronized void refreshTradingReadiness() {
228:         if(stopped || !source.enabled())return;
229:         List<String> symbols;
230:         try {symbols=List.copyOf(coins.enabledSymbols());}
231:         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
232:             for(String symbol:readinessSymbols)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED);
233:             log.error("[FIX-146][READINESS_INVENTORY_FAILED]",failure);return;
234:         }
235:         readinessSymbols=symbols;tradingReadiness.retainSymbols(symbols);
236:         boolean unscoped;
237:         try {unscoped=!readinessJdbc.queryForList(UNSCOPED_BLOCKERS).isEmpty()
238:                 || !readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id IS NOT NULL AND (symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(symbol)='') AND status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED') LIMIT 1").isEmpty();}
239:         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
240:             for(String symbol:symbols)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED);
241:             log.error("[FIX-146][READINESS_SCOPE_CHECK_FAILED]",failure);return;
242:         }
243:         long deadline=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(20);
244:         for(int checked=0;checked<symbols.size();checked++) {
245:             if(checked>0 && System.nanoTime()>=deadline)break;
246:             String symbol=symbols.get(Math.floorMod(readinessCursor++,symbols.size()));
247:             if(unscoped)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.UNSCOPED_WORK);
248:             else if(!activationApproved)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.ACTIVATION_REQUIRED);
249:             else {
250:                 try {
251:                     if(!readinessJdbc.queryForList(SYMBOL_LEGACY_BLOCKERS,symbol).isEmpty()) {
252:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.LEGACY_WORK_REVIEW);continue;
253:                     }
254:                     // Inconsistent cross-symbol ownership blocks both affected symbols.
255:                     var owners=readinessJdbc.queryForList("""
256:                         SELECT w.signal_id FROM signal_processing_work w
257:                         LEFT JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id
258:                         WHERE (w.symbol=? OR d.symbol=?) AND w.source_event_id IS NOT NULL
259:                           AND w.status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED')
260:                           AND (w.status='OPERATOR_CANCELLED' OR d.source_event_id IS NULL OR w.symbol IS NULL
261:                             OR w.interval_code IS NULL OR w.candle_open_time IS NULL OR d.symbol IS NULL
262:                             OR d.interval_code IS NULL OR d.candle_open_time IS NULL OR d.closed IS NULL
263:                             OR d.analysis_status IS NULL OR w.symbol<>d.symbol
264:                             OR w.interval_code<>d.interval_code OR w.candle_open_time<>d.candle_open_time
265:                             OR d.closed=0 OR d.analysis_status NOT IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED','EXECUTION_RETRY'))
266:                         LIMIT 1
267:                         """,symbol,symbol);
268:                     if(!owners.isEmpty()) {recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.OWNER_INCONSISTENT);continue;}
269:                     if(automaticFirstCutoverEnabled)automaticActivation.activateFirst(symbol,intervals.getIntervals());
270:                     var validated=validateSymbolCutover(symbol);
271:                     if(!fix142Enabled && !readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT symbol FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? AND active_delivery_state<>'NONE'",symbol).isEmpty()) {
272:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.OWNERSHIP_MODE_MISMATCH);continue;
273:                     }
274:                     if(fix142Enabled && !readinessJdbc.queryForList("""
275:                         SELECT d.source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery d WHERE d.symbol=? AND phase BETWEEN 1 AND 3
276:                         AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shared_market_consumer_state s WHERE s.symbol=d.symbol
277:                           AND s.active_delivery_event_id=d.source_event_id AND s.active_delivery_state IN ('ACTIVE','REVIEW_REQUIRED')) LIMIT 1
278:                         """,symbol).isEmpty()) {
279:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.PARTIAL_DELIVERY);continue;
280:                     }
281:                     // Avoid periodic INSERT/locking for already published history. A changed
282:                     // boundary is revalidated next pass; dispatch still checks durable approval.
283:                     Integer history=readinessJdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at=? AND cutover_sequence=?",Integer.class,
284:                         symbol,validated.get("cutover_at"),validated.get("cutover_sequence"));
285:                     Boolean stillApproved=history!=null && history>0;
286:                     if(!stillApproved)stillApproved=readinessTransaction.execute(tx->{
287:                         var state=readinessJdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? FOR UPDATE",symbol);
288:                         if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(state))return false;
289:                         for(String field:List.of("cutover_at","cutover_sequence","approved_sequence","approved_cutover_at","approved_at","approved_by","approval_reference"))
290:                             if(!Objects.equals(state.get(field),validated.get(field)))return false;
291:                         readinessJdbc.update("INSERT IGNORE INTO shared_market_cutover_history(symbol,cutover_at,cutover_sequence) VALUES(?,?,?)",
292:                             symbol,state.get("cutover_at"),state.get("cutover_sequence"));return true;
293:                     });
294:                     recordReadiness(symbol,Boolean.TRUE.equals(stillApproved)?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.READY:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED);
295:                 } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
296:                     recordReadiness(symbol,readinessReason(failure));
297:                     log.debug("[FIX-146][READINESS_CHECK_FAILED] symbol={}",symbol,failure);
298:                 }
299:             }
300:             if(System.nanoTime()>=deadline)break;
301:         }
302:     }
303:     private SharedTradingReadiness.Reason readinessReason(RuntimeException failure) {
304:         String message=String.valueOf(failure.getMessage());
305:         if(message.contains("missing/unapproved/review-required cutover"))return automaticFirstCutoverEnabled
306:             ?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.AUTOMATIC_ACTIVATION_WAITING:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED;
307:         if(message.contains("missing approval audit"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.APPROVAL_AUDIT_MISSING;
308:         if(message.contains("invalid sequence boundary"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.INVALID_BOUNDARY;
309:         if(message.contains("source sequence regressed"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_REGRESSED;
310:         if(message.contains("incomplete source history"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_HISTORY_MISSING;
311:         return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED;
312:     }
313:     private void recordReadiness(String symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason reason) {
314:         var before=tradingReadiness.record(symbol,reason);
315:         if(before==null || before.reason()!=reason) {
316:             Fix142Metrics.count("READINESS_"+reason.name());
317:             log.warn("[FIX-146][SYMBOL_READINESS] symbol={}, reason={}, checkedUtc={}, checkedKsa={}; audited first-activation policy, no uncertain retry",
318:                 symbol,reason,Instant.now(),Instant.now().atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)));
319:         }
320:     }
321: 
322:     public void discover() {
323:         if(stopped || "OFF".equals(source.mode())) return;
324:         try {
325:             for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) {
326:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol))continue;
327:                 var state=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
328:                 if(state.isEmpty()) {
329:                     if(source.enabled()) { log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}; explicit cutover boundary missing",symbol); continue; }
330:                     var seq=source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT last_sequence FROM market_data_stream_cursor WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
331:                     if(seq.isEmpty()) continue;
332:                     long n=((Number)seq.getFirst().get("last_sequence")).longValue();
333:                     jdbc.update("INSERT IGNORE INTO shared_market_consumer_state(symbol,cutover_sequence,cutover_at,discovered_sequence,status,cutover_source) VALUES(?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),?,'PENDING_CUTOVER','AUTO_OBSERVED')",symbol,n,n);
334:                     continue;
335:                 }
336:                 if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state.getFirst(),source.enabled())) continue;
337:                 int pending=fix142Enabled?-1:jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4",Integer.class,symbol);
338:                 boolean discoveryHeartbeat=System.nanoTime()-discoveryLogTimes.getOrDefault(symbol,0L)>=TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30);
339:                 if(discoveryHeartbeat)discoveryLogTimes.put(symbol,System.nanoTime());
340:                 if(!fix142Enabled && pending>=200) {
341:                     if(discoveryHeartbeat)log.warn("[FIX-132][CONSUMER_LAG] symbol={}, pending={}; discovery paused before analysis admission",symbol,pending);
342:                     continue;
343:                 }
344:                 long after=((Number)state.getFirst().get("discovered_sequence")).longValue();
345:                 var rows=source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT * FROM market_data_stream_event WHERE symbol=? AND symbol_sequence>? ORDER BY symbol_sequence LIMIT ?",symbol,after,fix142Enabled?32:Math.min(100,200-pending));
346:                 log.debug("[FIX-137][DISCOVERY_BATCH] symbol={}, afterSequence={}, pending={}, fetched={}",
347:                     symbol,after,pending,rows.size());
348:                 if(discoveryHeartbeat)log.info("[FIX-138][DISCOVERY_PROGRESS] symbol={}, afterSequence={}, pendingPricePhases={}, fetched={}, lastFetchedObservationUtc={}",
349:                     symbol,after,pending,rows.size(),rows.isEmpty()?null:time(rows.getLast(),"observed_at"));
350:                 // Shared connection is returned before any local transaction starts.
351:                 transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
352:                     var locked=lockSymbol(symbol);
353:                     if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(locked,source.enabled()) || ((Number)locked.get("discovered_sequence")).longValue()!=after) return;
354:                     long next=after;
355:                     Map<String,Integer> batchSlots=new HashMap<>();
356:                     for(var e:rows) {
357:                         long sequence=num(e,"symbol_sequence");
358:                         if(sequence!=next+1) {
359:                             jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET status='SEQUENCE_GAP' WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
360:                             log.error("[FIX-132][SEQUENCE_GAP] symbol={}, expected={}, found={}",symbol,next+1,sequence);break;
361:                         }
362:                         if(fix142Enabled) {
363:                             String interval=str(e,"interval_code");
364:                             int available=batchSlots.computeIfAbsent(interval,key->Math.max(0,protocol.ceiling-protocol.reserve-protocol.exposure(symbol,key,Instant.now())));
365:                             var admission=protocol.admit(e,symbol,available>0);
366:                             if(admission==Fix142DeliveryProtocol.AdmissionResult.NO_CAPACITY)break;
367:                             if(admission==Fix142DeliveryProtocol.AdmissionResult.INSERTED)batchSlots.put(interval,available-1); // duplicates validated; checkpoint remains contiguous
368:                         } else {
369:                         jdbc.update("""
370:                             INSERT INTO shared_market_event_delivery(source_event_id,symbol,symbol_sequence,interval_code,
371:                             candle_open_time,candle_close_time,closed,observed_at,received_at,source_created_at,price,source,classification)
372:                             VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
373:                             """,e.get("id"),symbol,sequence,e.get("interval_code"),e.get("candle_open_time"),e.get("candle_close_time"),
374:                             e.get("closed"),e.get("observed_at"),e.get("received_at"),e.get("created_at"),e.get("price"),e.get("source"),e.get("classification"));
375:                         }
376:                         next=sequence;
377:                     }
378:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET discovered_sequence=? WHERE symbol=?",next,symbol);
379:                 });
380:             }
381:             sourceStatus("AVAILABLE",null);
382:         } catch(Exception e) {
383:             try { sourceStatus("SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE",shortError(e)); } catch(Exception diagnostic) { log.error("[FIX-132][HEALTH_RECORD_FAILED]",diagnostic); }
384:             long now=System.nanoTime();
385:             if(now-lastSourceLog>TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5)) {
386:                 lastSourceLog=now;log.error("[FIX-132][SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE] discovery failed; checkpoint not silently advanced",e);
387:             }
388:         }
389:     }
390:     public void dispatchPrices() {
391:         if(stopped || "OFF".equals(source.mode())) return;
392:         try {
393:             // At most TWO submitted tasks, so newFixedThreadPool's internal queue cannot grow.
394:             List<String> symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();
395:             for(int checked=0;checked<symbols.size();checked++) {
396:                 if(activePrices.size()>=2) break;
397:                 String symbol=symbols.get(Math.floorMod(nextSymbol++,symbols.size()));
398:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !activePrices.add(symbol))continue;
399:                 try { prices.execute(()->{try {
400:                     int count=fix142Enabled?jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4 LIMIT 8",symbol).size():jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4",Integer.class,symbol);
401:                     for(int n=0;n<Math.min(8,count)&&!stopped;n++) processNext(symbol);
402:                 } finally { activePrices.remove(symbol); }}); }
403:                 catch(RuntimeException rejected) { activePrices.remove(symbol); throw rejected; }
404:             }
405:         } catch(Exception e) { log.error("[FIX-132][DISPATCH_FAILED]",e); }
406:     }
407:     private Map<String,Object> lockSymbol(String symbol) {
408:         return jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? FOR UPDATE",symbol);
409:     }
410:     private boolean beginProtection(String symbol,long id,String attempt) {
411:         return Boolean.TRUE.equals(transaction.execute(tx->{
412:             var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
413:             if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,source.enabled()))return false;
414:             var event=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
415:             if(num(event,"phase")!=1 || !"1m".equals(event.get("interval_code")))return true;
416:             if(event.get("protection_started_at")==null) {
417:                 // Durable START precedes business. A process crash cannot turn an
418:                 // already-started wallet attempt into a fresh automatic retry.
419:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_started_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),protection_owner=?,status='PROTECTION_RUNNING' WHERE source_event_id=?",attempt,id);
420:                 return true;
421:             }
422:             if(attempt.equals(event.get("protection_owner")))return true;
423:             // Never steal/re-execute a started attempt. After 30s, fence it into
424:             // review; a worker already in its transaction holds this row through
425:             // commit, while one resuming later must observe REVIEW_REQUIRED.
426:             if(time(event,"protection_started_at").plusSeconds(30).isBefore(Instant.now())) {
427:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED',last_error='Started protection outcome requires reconciliation; no crash retry' WHERE source_event_id=?",id);
428:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
429:                 log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}, event={}; previous protection attempt unconfirmed",symbol,id);
430:             }
431:             return false;
432:         }));
433:     }
434:     void processNext(String symbol) {
435:         if(!symbolAllowed(symbol))return;
436:         Long id=null;
437:         try(var timing=com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource.context("symbol="+symbol+" phase=SELECT")) {
438:             var candidates=fix142Enabled ? transaction.execute(tx->{
439:                 var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
440:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,source.enabled()))return List.<Map<String,Object>>of();
441:                 var selected=protocol.select(symbol,state);
442:                 return selected==null?List.<Map<String,Object>>of():List.of(selected);
443:             }) : jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4 ORDER BY symbol_sequence LIMIT 1",symbol);
444:             if(candidates.isEmpty())return;
445:             id=num(candidates.getFirst(),"source_event_id");
446:             log.debug("[FIX-137][DELIVERY_START] event={}, symbol={}",id,symbol);
447:             final long eventId=id;
448:             final String attemptToken=fix142Enabled?jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT active_delivery_owner_token FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",String.class,symbol):UUID.randomUUID().toString();
449:             // Small bounded turn: one event, four independently committed phases.
450:             for(int stage=0;stage<4;stage++) {
451:                 final int expected=stage;
452:                 if(source.enabled() && expected==1) {
453:                     if(fix142Enabled) {
454:                         ProtectionClaim result=claimProtectionAttempt(symbol,eventId,attemptToken);
455:                         if(result==ProtectionClaim.BLOCKED || result==ProtectionClaim.REVIEW)return;
456:                         if(result==ProtectionClaim.CLAIMED && !prepareProtectionApply(symbol,eventId,attemptToken))return;
457:                     } else if(!beginProtection(symbol,eventId,attemptToken))return;
458:                 }
459:                 int attempts=0;
460:                 while(true) {
461:                 try(var phaseTiming=com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource.context("symbol="+symbol+" event="+eventId+" phase="+expected);var phaseSpan=Fix142Metrics.phase(symbol,eventId,expected)) {
462:                 transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
463:                     var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
464:                     if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,source.enabled())) {
465:                         if(fix142Enabled&&expected==1)restoreUnenteredClaim(eventId,attemptToken);
466:                         return;
467:                     }
468:                     if(fix142Enabled && !protocol.owned(state,eventId,attemptToken)) {
469:                         if(expected==1)restoreUnenteredClaim(eventId,attemptToken);
470:                         return;
471:                     }
472:                     var e=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",eventId);
473:                     if(num(e,"phase")!=expected || "REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(e.get("status"))) {
474:                         if(fix142Enabled&&expected==1)restoreUnenteredClaim(eventId,attemptToken);
475:                         return;
476:                     }
477:                     if(source.enabled() && expected==1 && "1m".equals(e.get("interval_code"))
478:                             && e.get("protection_completed_at")==null && !attemptToken.equals(e.get("protection_owner")))return;
479:                     if(!source.enabled()) {
480:                         jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET phase=4,status='OBSERVED',analysis_status='OBSERVED' WHERE source_event_id=?",eventId);return;
481:                     }
482:                     boolean postCutover=num(e,"symbol_sequence")>num(state,"cutover_sequence")
483:                         && time(e,"observed_at")!=null && time(e,"observed_at").isAfter(time(state,"cutover_at"));
484:                     boolean canonical="1m".equals(e.get("interval_code"));
485:                     String reason = protectionReason(symbol,state,e,Instant.now());
486:                     boolean eligible="ELIGIBLE".equals(reason);
487:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET owner_token=?,status='PROCESSING' WHERE source_event_id=?",owner,eventId);
488:                     if(expected==0 && canonical && e.get("observed_at")!=null) {
489:                         jdbc.update("""
490:                             INSERT IGNORE INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,source_event_id,source_sequence,source_received_at,delivery_status)
491:                             VALUES(?,?,?,'COLLECTOR_KLINE_LIVE',?,?,?,'PENDING')
492:                             """,symbol,e.get("observed_at"),e.get("price"),eventId,e.get("symbol_sequence"),e.get("received_at"));
493:                     }

26: @Component
27: @Order(10)
28: @RequiredArgsConstructor
29: @Slf4j
30: public class MarketDataBootstrapService implements ApplicationRunner {
31:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("#{'${shared-market.mode:OFF}' == 'LIVE'}")
32:     private boolean sharedMarketEnabled;
33:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}")
34:     private String marketMode="OFF";
35: 
36: 
37:     private static final int REQUIRED_CANDLES = 300;
38: 
39:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties marketDataProperties;
40:     private final TradingProperties tradingProperties;
41:     private final CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService;
42:     private final MarketDataService marketDataService;
43:     private final TechnicalIndicatorService technicalIndicatorService;
44:     private final AnalysisService analysisService;
45:     private final PaperTradingService paperTradingService;
46:     private final TradeSignalRepository tradeSignalRepository;
47: 
48:     @Override
49:     public void run(ApplicationArguments args) {
50:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
51:         if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
52:                 || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
53:             log.info("Historical candle bootstrap is disabled");
54:             return;
55:         }
56: 
57:         List<String> symbols = selectSymbols();
58:         List<String> intervals = selectIntervals();
59:         int limit = Math.max(
60:                 REQUIRED_CANDLES,
61:                 Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
62:         );
63: 
64:         for (String symbol : symbols) {
65:             for (String interval : intervals) {
66:                 bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
67:             }
68:         }
69:     }
70: 
71: 
72:     public void bootstrapSymbol(String rawSymbol) {
73:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
74:         if (!marketDataProperties.isEnabled()
75:                 || !marketDataProperties.getHistorical().isEnabled()) {
76:             log.info("Historical candle bootstrap skipped for {} because historical loading is disabled", rawSymbol);
77:             return;
78:         }
79: 
80:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase();
81:         if (symbol.isBlank()) {
82:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
83:         }
84: 
85:         int limit = Math.max(
86:                 REQUIRED_CANDLES,
87:                 Math.min(1000, marketDataProperties.getHistorical().getLimit())
88:         );
89:         for (String interval : selectIntervals()) {
90:             bootstrap(symbol, interval, limit);
91:         }
92:     }
93: 
94:     private void bootstrap(String symbol, String interval, int limit) {
95:         try {
96:             int inserted = marketDataService.importCandles(symbol, interval, limit);
97: 
98:             log.info(
99:                     "Historical bootstrap completed: symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}",
100:                     symbol,
101:                     interval,
102:                     inserted
103:             );
104: 
105:             // FIX-153: bootstrap is data only. An imported close must never seed a signal
106:             // or execute a STARTUP trade; only this trader's runtime closes qualify in OFF.
107:             if("OFF".equals(marketMode)){
108:                 log.info("[FIX-153][BACKFILL_IMPORT_RETURNED] symbol={}, interval={}, inserted={}, analysis=false, execution=false",symbol,interval,inserted);
109:                 return;
110:             }
111:             TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
112:                     .calculateAndPersist(symbol, interval, null)
113:                     .orElse(null);
114: 
115:             if (indicator == null) {
116:                 log.warn(
117:                         "Initial indicator skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, reason=fewer than 210 closed candles",
118:                         symbol,
119:                         interval
120:                 );
121:                 return;
122:             }
123: 
124:             // FIX-072: bootstrap can overlap with the live candle-close worker during startup.
125:             // Never regenerate or re-execute a signal that Production already persisted for this
126:             // exact symbol/interval/candle; the unique key remains the final database safety net.
127:             if (tradeSignalRepository.existsBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
128:                     indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime())) {
129:                 log.info(
130:                         "Initial signal skipped: already exists for symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}",
131:                         indicator.getSymbol(), indicator.getIntervalCode(), indicator.getCandleOpenTime());
132:                 return;
133:             }
134: 
135:             TradeSignal signal = analysisService.analyzeForProcessing(indicator, com.crypto.execution.processing.ProcessingOrigin.STARTUP);
136:             paperTradingService.processSignal(signal);
137: 
138:             log.info(
139:                     "Initial signal generated: symbol={}, interval={}, score={}, decision={}",
140:                     symbol,
141:                     interval,
142:                     signal.getTotalScore(),
143:                     signal.getDecision()
144:             );
145:         } catch (Exception exception) {
146:             log.error(
147:                     "Historical bootstrap failed: symbol={}, interval={}",
148:                     symbol,
149:                     interval,
150:                     exception
151:             );
152:         }
153:     }

110:             // After bounded shutdown, even the unresolved CURRENT handshake retains a cleanup path.

112:             if (closeWorker.isShutdown() && handler.gate.isDrained()) handler.close();

122:     private List<Candidate> openCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
123:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
124:         for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
125:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
126:             Instant lower=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window);
127:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
128:             Instant latestOpen=eligible.minusSeconds(seconds);
129:             result.addAll(jdbc.query("""
130:                 SELECT symbol,interval_code,open_time FROM candle
131:                 WHERE closed=0 AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<=?
132:                   AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
